Let's derive a powerful active-set algorithm:

Let's design final program:

Global parent accessible as `height_sequence_1` (maybe not defined? The prompt says you can access through global variable. It may be available. We'll try except).

`propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800)`:
- set deadline = start + budget_s - 30 (safe).
- initialize best_seq, best_score.
- Load parent, evaluate using internal `_score` (same formula as evaluator). If valid, update.
- Run several phases:
  1. Dense optimizer (modified L-BFGS continuation + Newton) on parent for maybe 500s.
  2. Sparse support construction for multiple m/n (maybe 600s). For each support found, optimize weights; update best.
  3. Hybrid: take best sparse support and maybe allow dense L-BFGS on full sequence to fill zeros? Or take best dense and sample high coefficients as support? 
  4. Final polish best sequence with dense L-BFGS small beta, and weight-specific if sparse.
- Return best as list floats.

Let's design active set Newton better:
- Given a (nonnegative sum1), compute c, max M.
- Active mask: c >= M*(1-tol) - epsilon. But need exact active set at optimum maybe unknown. We can use a dual approach: solve for a direction d that reduces all active peaks and increases sum? Normalize.
- The previous Newton with uniform active weights computes H from uniform active average. Step dx = -H^{-1} grad_avg? But to reduce max, we need choose active weights w such that gradient g=2corr(a,w) aligns with a descent direction. At optimum, there exists w in active cone with g_i = const for positive support. We can approximate by solving for w (nonnegative over coarse active mask) that makes g as constant as possible, then H = 2corr(.,w) and solve. The uniform is one choice; we could choose w by a least problem: minimize ∥2corr(a,w)-mean∥^2 subject w≥0, ∑w=1 over A. This is a QP in w of size |A|, can solve via scipy? For |A| maybe hundreds, m? Objective matrix Gram = 4 C_{ij} where (corr(a, e_i))^T corr(a,e_j). Could be large but A small. Since A support c near max maybe hundreds. We can solve using projected gradient / simplex. But w also appears in H for step. Complex.

- Candidate new positions: For a chosen high sum k and chosen point u (partner v), moving u changes count at k minus: if u==v, decrement 1 at 2u; else decrement 2 at k (because ordered pairs (u,v),(v,u)).      old_m = max_count(heap)

- Get k = top heap position with c[k]=cur_max (if cur_max > target_max maybe; if cur_max <= target_max still optimize energy, maybe pick random high).

Implement batch check:
```
def _check_candidates(X, S_arr, c, R):
    valid = ~inS[X] perhaps
    if len(S_arr)==0: return valid
    # For each x, need max over s of c[x+s] <= R-2
    # Use broadcasting may allocate B*k; B=256,k=300=76800 ints, okay.
    offsets = X[:,None] + S_arr[None,:]
    # Ensure offsets within [0,2n-2]; x<n,s<n => x+s <=2n-2 okay.
    vals = c[offsets]
    maxvals = vals.max(axis=1)
    diag = c[2*X]
    valid = (maxvals <= R-2) & (diag <= R-1)
    return valid
```

Dynamic forbidden for greedy (counts only increase): For current counts and S, a position x is valid if:
- for all s∈S, c[x+s] <= R-2
- c[2x] <= R-1
Let `bad_off = {t: c[t] >= R-1}` (since adding offdiag would make >R if old+2>R). Then x invalid if ∃s∈S with x+s∈bad_off. This is Boolean convolution (OR) of inS with bad_off. We can maintain an integer `forbid_count[x]` = number of s∈S such that x+s∈bad_off. Valid if forbid_count[x]==0 and diag_ok. When counts change from not bad to bad (cross threshold R-1), for that t, we need increment forbid_count[t-s] for all s∈S. Also when S grows, for each existing bad t, increment forbid_count[t-new_s]. Let's design dynamic forbidden for greedy more precisely.

State:
- c counts int.
- S_arr current (size k).
- inS bool.
- `bad_off` boolean length C: c[t] >= R-1? For offdiag additions, if old count >= R-1, then any x with x+s=t invalid.

Implementation of greedy top-level:
```
def _greedy_sparse(n, m, R, seed, time_limit=None):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    C = 2*n-1
    c = np.zeros(C, dtype=np.int32)
    inS = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    S_list = []  # Python list for iteration? But we need np array for vector operations.
    S_arr = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    bad_off = np.zeros(C, dtype=bool)
    bad_positions = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    forbid = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    diag_bad = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    for k in range(m):
       if time_limit...
       valid_mask = (~inS) & (forbid == 0) & (~diag_bad)
       idxs = np.where(valid_mask)[0]
       if idxs.size == 0: return None, None
       x = int(idxs[rng.integers(idxs.size)])
       # update counts
       changed = np.empty(k+1, dtype=np.int64)
       if k>0:
          changed[:k] = x + S_arr
          delta = np.empty(k+1,dtype=np.int32); delta[:k]=2; delta[k]=1; changed[k]=2*x
       else:
          changed[0]=2*x; delta=np.array([1],dtype=np.int32)
       old = c[changed].astype(np.int32).copy()
       new = old + delta
       c[changed] = new
       # diag_bad
       even = changed[changed % 2 == 0]
       if even.size:
          y = even//2
          mask = (new[(changed//2==y)?])? Need align.
```

```
Propose_candidate outline:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
  t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  best_seq, best_score = None, np.inf
  # load parent
  parent = ...
  if parent valid: update.
  # quick dense L-BFGS for maybe 200s? But if sparse works, maybe not needed.
  # Try sparse construction first because likely fast and promising.
  sparse_deadline = deadline - 300  # leave dense fallback/polish
  for R,m in combos:
      if time > sparse_deadline: break
      for seed in [seed, seed+1, seed+2]:
         S = _greedy_sparse(n_base, m, R, seed)
         if S is None: continue
         S_shift, n_new = trim
         # compute uniform sequence, score
         seq = zeros(n_new); seq[S_shift]=1/m
         sc=_score(seq)
         update
         # if score good, optimize weights for this support
         opt_seq, opt_sc = _sparse_weight_opt(S_shift, n_new, time_limit=maybe 60)
         update
      if best_score < 1.5028: break # below target
  # If best_score still >1.5027, run dense optimizer from parent and maybe from sparse best (if length not too large)
  if time < deadline - 100: run dense L-BFGS on parent; update
  # run dense polish on best_seq (if length <=32768); update
  # final return best
```

Need ensure if sparse succeeds with score ~1.505, dense can improve. But dense on sparse sequence with many zeros may be bad; we can instead run sparse L-BFGS more.

Let's implement `_sparse_weight_opt(S, n, deadline, min_time_remaining=...)`:
- m=len(S). If m<10 maybe skip.
- Initial z = zeros(m) (uniform). Set globals: `_SPARSE_OPT['sum_flat']=sum_flat`, `n`, `C`.

For dense L-BFGS, we can implement similar but perhaps reuse `_lbfgs_continuation` from previous? Let's write a simpler top-level:
```
def _dense_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=60.0, max_beta_stages=20):
    n=len(a_init); a=copy; a/max(sum); z=log(a); beta schedule from gap.
    for beta in betas:
       if time remaining < min_time_remaining: break
       _DENSE_OPT['n']=n; _DENSE_OPT['beta']=beta
       res=minimize(_dense_obj, z, jac=_dense_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options...)
       a_new=softmax(res.x); sc=_score(a_new); if sc<best: update
       z=log(a_new)
    return best_a
```

`_dense_lse_grad`: need compute h using `_corr_a_A(a, lam, n)`. Let's implement top-level:
```
def _corr_a_A(a, A_vec, n):
    flip_a = a[::-1]
    out_len = n + len(A_vec) - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(flip_a, N)
    fA = np.fft.rfft(A_vec, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa*fA, N)
    return r[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Let's implement greedy as:
```
def _greedy_sparse_cap(n, m_target, R, seed, max_sample=64, time_limit=None):
   ... for k in range(m_target):
      valid_indices = np.where(...)
      if empty: break
      # sample candidates from valid_indices (if many, choose max_sample)
      if valid_indices.size > max_sample:
          sample = valid_indices[rng.choice(valid_indices.size, max_sample, replace=False)]
      else sample = valid_indices
      # choose best sample by objective
      best_x=None; best_score...
      for x in sample:
          off = x + S_arr
          old_off = c[off]
          new_off = old_off + 2
          old_diag = c[2*x]
          new_diag = old_diag + 1
          newmax = max(new_off.max(), new_diag)
          # energy inc: off 4*old+4, diag 2*old+1
          einc = 4*old_off.sum() + 4*k + 2*old_diag + 1
          # penalize new bad creations? newmax enough
          # maybe also Penalize if x is close to existing? no.
          sc = newmax*1e6 + einc
          if sc < best: ...
      # But if best newmax > R? valid should ensure <=R? For off, valid ensures old max <=R-2; diag old<=R-1, so newmax<=R.
      add best.
   return S_arr

def _dense_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=30.0, max_stages=25, label=""):
   n=len(a_init); a=np.clip(a_init,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<... return
   a=a/s; best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30)); z-=z.mean()
   c0=_full_convolve(a,a); cmax0=max
   # sort to estimate beta start
   sorted=np.sort(c0)[::-1]; gap = cmax0 - sorted[min(100,len(sorted)-1)]
   beta_start = max(cmax0*0.02, gap*0.5) maybe? 
   beta_end = max(cmax0*1e-7, 1e-30)
   log_betas=np.linspace(np.log(beta_start), np.log(beta_end), max_stages)
   for i,beta in enumerate(log_betas):
      if time.monotonic()+min_time_remaining > deadline: break
      _DENSE_OPT[...]
      est_time=0.005*(n/16384)
      max_iter=int(min(3000, max(20, (deadline - time.monotonic() - min_time_remaining)/(est_time*8))))
      res=minimize(... options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-11,'maxcor':100,'maxls':100})
      a_new=softmax(res.x)
      sc=_score(a_new)
      if sc<best_sc: update
      z=log(a_new); z-=mean
      print...
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Let's implement and test mentally:
Greedy dynamic valid update order in function `_greedy_sparse_cap`:
Variables: `S_arr` old before add. `bad_positions` old. For candidate scoring, c currently consistent before add and valid ensures newmax<=R. Choose x.
Processing add:
```
old_bad_positions = bad_positions.copy() if size else empty
# update counts
if k>0:
   off = x + S_arr
   changed = np.concatenate([off, [2*x]])
   delta = np.concatenate([np.full(k,2), [1]])
else: changed=[2x], delta=[1]
old_counts = c[changed].copy()
c[changed] += delta
new_counts = c[changed]
# diag_bad update
for pos, newv in zip(changed,new_counts): if pos%2==0 and newv >= R: diag_bad[pos//2]=True
# identify newly bad positions: oldv < R-1 and newv >= R-1
new_bad_list=[]
for pos,oldv,newv in zip(changed, old_counts, new_counts):
   if oldv < R-1 and newv >= R-1: new_bad_list.append(pos)
# existing bad contributions for new point x: for t in old_bad_positions, forbid[t-x] +=1
if old_bad_positions.size:
   xs = old_bad_positions - x
   mask=(xs>=0)&(xs<n)
   np.add.at(forbid, xs[mask], 1)  # using add.at to be safe duplicates? xs distinct but ok.
# S_arr_new
S_arr_new = np.concatenate([S_arr, [x]])
# newly bad contributions for all S_arr_new
if new_bad_list:
   new_bad = np.array(new_bad_list)
   bad_off[new_bad]=True
   # For each t in new_bad:

       xs = t - S_arr_new
       mask=(xs>=0)&(xs<n)
       if mask.any(): np.add.at(forbid, xs[mask], 1)
   bad_positions = np.concatenate([bad_positions, new_bad])
inS[x]=True
S_arr = S_arr_new

def _binary_counts(S,n):

   if len(S)==0: return c
   idx = S[:,None] + S[None,:]
   np.add.at(c, idx.ravel(), 1)
   return c

def _dense_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=20.0, max_stages=22, label=""):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    a=np.asarray(a_init,float).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: a=np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    else: a=a/s
    n=len(a)
    if n <2: ...
    _DENSE_OPT['n']=n
    _DENSE_OPT['Nf']=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    _DENSE_OPT['Nc']=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))
    z-=z.mean()
    c0=_full_convolve(a,a)
    cmax0=float(c0.max())
    sorted_c=np.sort(c0)[::-1]
    gap = cmax0 - sorted_c[min(20, len(sorted_c)-1)]
    beta_start = max(cmax0*0.02, gap*0.25, 1e-30)
    beta_end = max(cmax0*1e-7, 1e-30)
    if beta_start <= beta_end: beta_start=beta_end*10
    n_beta = max(2, int(max_stages))
    log_betas=np.linspace(np.log(beta_start), np.log(beta_end), n_beta)
    last_beta=None; no_improve=0
    for i,beta in enumerate(log_betas):
        if beta <=0 or beta==last_beta: continue
        remaining=deadline - time.monotonic() - min_time_remaining
        if remaining < 3: break
        est = 0.002 + 0.004*(n/16384.0)
        # Each L-BFGS iteration may call obj/grad multiple; use 10*est per iter
        max_iter = int(min(5000, max(30, remaining/(est*10))))
        _DENSE_OPT['beta']=float(beta)
        try:
           res = minimize(_dense_obj, z, jac=_dense_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': max_iter, 'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-11,'maxcor':100,'maxls':100})
        except Exception as e: print; continue
        a_new=_softmax(res.x)
        sc=_score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy(); no_improve=0
           print
        else:
           no_improve+=1
           # still update z? maybe use res.x anyway for continuation even if score worse? Use better? Use result x if finite, but if score worse maybe keep previous.
           # For continuation, use previous best? If no improve, maybe use res.x? Could move to bad basin. Use best_a for z.
        z=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-30)); z-=z.mean()
        if no_improve >= 6: break
        last_beta=beta
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But if a beta stage does not improve, using best_a for z stalls. Could use res.x if it is finite and not inf to continue, even if score worse, to explore; but risk. Maybe use a mixed z: if score <= best_sc + small, use res.x; else best. For global, maybe resume from best. We'll use best.

`_full_convolve(a,a)` uses global N? Define:
```
def _full_convolve(a,b,N=None):
   out_n=len(a)+len(b)-1
   if N is None: N=_next_pow2(out_n)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out_n]
```
In dense obj, use Nf to avoid next. But `_full_convolve` with N optional.
```
def _dense_obj(z):
   n=_DENSE_OPT['n']; beta=_DENSE_OPT['beta']; N=_DENSE_OPT['Nf']
   a=_softmax(z)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   shifted=(c-cmax)/beta
   e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700, 0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): val=2*n*cmax
   else: val=2*n*(cmax + beta*np.log(s))
   return float(val)
```
Gradient:
```
def _dense_grad(z):
   n=...; beta...; N_c...
   a=_softmax(z)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nf); c=irfft...; cmax...; e...; lam=e/s if s>... else onehot
   flip=a[::-1]
   # h_i = ∑ a_j lam_{i+j}
   # conv(flip_a, lam) slice n-1:2n-1 with Nc
   fl=np.fft.rfft(flip,Nc); flam=np.fft.rfft(lam,Nc); r=np.fft.irfft(fl*flam,Nc)
   h=r[n-1:2*n-1]
   W=float(np.dot(lam,c))
   grad=4.0*n*a*(h-W)
   return grad
```
Need ensure `Nc` set. In `_dense_lbfgs`, set both. For small n, okay.

`_softmax` top-level:
```
def _softmax(z):
 z=z-np.max(z)
 e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,700))
 s=e.sum()
 if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
 return e/s

a=np.maximum(a,0)
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-15: return inf
c=_full_convolve(a,a)
return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/ (s*s))

      update
      z...

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(10, float(budget_s) - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed)%...)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    parent=None; parent_len=0
    try: ...
    if parent is not None: best=try_update(...parent)
    else: best=try_update(np.ones(16)/16,'init')
    final_n = len(parent) if parent else 16384
    if final_n < 16: final_n=16
    # if parent length > 32768? cap? If parent length huge, dense expensive. But previous 16384.
    if final_n > 32768:
       final_n = 32768
       parent = _interpolate_seq(parent, final_n)
    # small multires
    current = np.ones(4)/4.0
    best, score = _try_update(best, score, current, "n4")
    small_targets = [8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096]
    small_limit = min(hard_deadline - 550.0, t_start + 260.0)
    idx=0
    for target in small_targets:
       idx+=1
       if time.monotonic() >= small_limit or hard_deadline - time.monotonic() < 450: break
       # local end: if remaining small budget, split among remaining targets
       remain_targets = len(small_targets)-idx+1
       local_end = min(small_limit, time.monotonic() + max(3.0, (small_limit - time.monotonic())/remain_targets))
       a = _interpolate_seq(current,target)
       # if target==4096 maybe allocate more? formula okay.
       a, sc = _dense_lbfgs(a, local_end, min_time_remaining=2.0, max_stages=24, label=f"small{target}")
       current = a
       best,best_score = _try_update(...)
    # full from coarse if time
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 400:
       a = _interpolate_seq(current, final_n)
       med_end = min(hard_deadline - 350, time.monotonic() + 350)
       a, sc = _dense_lbfgs(a, med_end, min_time_remaining=30, max_stages=24, label="full_from_mres")
       current = a; update
    # parent full if exists and score not too bad
    if parent is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 300:
       p = parent if len==final_n else interp
       p_end = min(hard_deadline - 250, time.monotonic() + 400)
       p_seq,sc=...
       update
    # sparse
    if time.monotonic() < hard_dense? -200:
       sparse_end = min(hard_deadline - 180, time.monotonic() + 90)
       s_seq, s_score = _run_sparse_experiment(min(final_n,32768), sparse_end, seed+12345)
       update
       if s_seq is not None and s_score < best_score + 0.02 and time < hard_deadline-100:
           # dense polish sparse sequence for 50s
           e=min(hard_deadline-80,time+50)
           dseq,sc=_dense_lbfgs(s_seq,e,min_time=15,max_stages=12,label="sparse_dense")
           update
    # final polish best
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10:
       e=min(hard_deadline-5,time.monotonic()+80)
       dseq,sc=_dense_lbfgs(best_seq,e,min_time=5,max_stages=10,label="final_polish")
       update
    # perturb maybe if time
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20:
       for sigma in (0.01,0.05):
          if time < hard_deadline-15:
             pseq=_perturb(best_seq, sigma, rng)
             e=min(hard_deadline-10,time+30)
             dseq,sc=_dense_lbfgs(pseq,e,min_time=3,max_stages=8,label=f"pert{sigma}")
             update
    # final safe
    if best_seq is None: best_seq=np.ones(16)/16
    best_seq=np.clip(...)
    if sum<0.01: fallback
    # maybe call evaluate_sequence? no need
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

```
   p_full = ...
   run
   # mix polish 120s if time
   mix = 0.5*p_full +0.5*_interpolate(current, final_n)
   run
```

```python
"""
Multi-scale log-sum-exp optimizer for nonnegative step functions.

The objective is
    2n * max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.
We use several complementary ideas:

1. A coarse-to-fine schedule:
   optimize small problems (n=4,8,...,4096) with very aggressive
   log-sum-exp continuation, then interpolate and continue at full size.

2. Several full-size starts:
   - parent incumbent, if available
   - the refined multi-scale sequence
   - a blend of the two
   - randomized perturbations of the current best.

3. Sparse additive-combinatorial starts:
   greedy construction of 0/1 supports with bounded pair-sum multiplicity,
   uniform weighting, sparse L-BFGS weight refinement, and optional dense
   relaxation from the resulting support.

All continuous optimization is done in log-softmax coordinates with a
log-sum-exp approximation to the maximum coefficient.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


_DENSE_OPT = {"beta": 0.0, "n": 0, "Nf": 0, "Nc": 0}
_SPARSE_OPT = {"sum_flat": None, "n": 0, "C": 0, "beta": 0.0}


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(z))
    return e / s


def _full_convolve(a, b, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if len(a) == 0 or len(b) == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    out_n = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:out_n]
    return r


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _full_convolve(a, a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def _interpolate_seq(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_old = a.size
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        b = a.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
        b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n_new)
    return b / s


def _perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
    z = z + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(z.size)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    return _softmax(z)


def _corr_a_A(a, A_vec, n, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    A_vec = np.asarray(A_vec, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    flip_a = a[::-1]
    out_len = n + len(A_vec) - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(flip_a, N)
    fA = np.fft.rfft(A_vec, N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fA, N)
    return r[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _dense_obj(z):
    n = _DENSE_OPT["n"]
    beta = float(_DENSE_OPT["beta"])
    Nf = int(_DENSE_OPT["Nf"])
    if n <= 1 or bet_acc <= 0.0:
        pass
    a = _softmax(z)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nf)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return float(2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * np.log(s)))


def _dense_grad(z):
    n = _DENSE_OPT["n"]
    beta = float(_DENSE_OPT["beta"])
    Nf = int(_DENSE_OPT["Nf"])
    Nc = int(_DENSE_OPT["Nc"])
    a = _softmax(z)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nf)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        lam = np.zeros_like(c)
        lam[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        lam = e / s

    h = _corr_a_A(a, lam, n, N=Nc)
    W = float(np.dot(lam, c))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - W)
    return grad


def _dense_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining=20.0, max_stages=22, label="dense"):
    t_start_here = time.monotonic()
    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        a = np.ones(4, dtype=np.float64) / 4.0
        n = 4
    if s < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(best_a)

    _DENSE_OPT["n"] = int(n)
    _DENSE_OPT["Nf"] = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    _DENSE_OPT["Nc"] = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-30))
    z = z - float(z.mean())

    c0 = _full_convolve(best_a, best_a)
    cmax0 = float(np.max(c0)) if c0.size else 0.0
    if cmax0 < 1e-300:
        return best_a, best_sc

    sorted_c = np.sort(c0)[::-1]
    gap = cmax0 - float(sorted_c[min(20, sorted_c.size - 1)])
    beta_start = max(cmax0 * 0.02, gap * 0.25, 1e-30)
    beta_end = max(cmax0 * 1e-7, 1e-30)
    if beta_start <= beta_end:
        beta_start = beta_end * 10.0

    log_betas = np.linspace(np.log(beta_start), np.log(beta_end), int(max(2, max_stages)))
    est = 0.005 + 0.006 * (float(n) / 16384.0)
    no_improve = 0

    for i in range(len(log_betas)):
        beta = float(log_betas[i])
        if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
            continue
        remaining = float(deadline) - time.monotonic() - float(min_time_remaining)
        if remaining < 3.0:
            break

        max_iter = int(min(1500, max(30, remaining / max(1e-9, est * 20.0))))
        _DENSE_OPT["beta"] = beta
        stage_start = time.monotonic()
        result = None
        try:
            result = minimize(
                _dense_obj,
                z,
                jac=_dense_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max_iter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "maxcor": 100,
                    "maxls": 100,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            result = None

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - stage_start
        if max_iter > 0:
            actual = elapsed / max_iter
            if actual > 0.0:
                est = max(est, actual)

        if result is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(result.x)):
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            a_new = _softmax(result.x)
            sc_new = _score(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = float(sc_new)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                print(f"  [{label}] beta={beta:.3e} score={best_sc:.12f} t={time.monotonic()-t_start_here:.1f}s")
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve == 1:
                    print(f"  [{label}] beta={beta:.3e} no improve, best={best_sc:.12f}")

        z = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-30))
        z = z - float(z.mean())

        if no_improve >= 6:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _binary_counts(S, n):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    C = max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    c = np.zeros(C, dtype=np.int64)
    if S.size == 0:
        return c
    idx = S[:, None] + S[None, :]
    np.add.at(c, idx.ravel(), 1)
    return c[: 2 * n - 1]


def _uniform_sparse_seq(S, n):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    m = S.size
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    a = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a[S] = 1.0 / float(m)
    return a


def _greedy_sparse_cap(n, m_target, R, seed, max_sample=48, time_limit=None):
    n = int(n)
    m_target = int(m_target)
    R = int(R)
    if n <= 1 or m_target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))
    C = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.zeros(C, dtype=np.int32)
    inS = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    S_arr = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
    bad_off = np.zeros(C, dtype=bool)
    bad_positions = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
    forbid = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
    diag_bad = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)

    for k in range(m_target):
        if time_limit is not None and time.monotonic() > float(time_limit):
            break

        valid_mask = (~inS) & (forbid == 0) & (~diag_bad)
        valid_indices = np.where(valid_mask)[0]
        if valid_indices.size == 0:
            break

        if valid_indices.size > max_sample:
            chosen_idx = rng.integers(0, valid_indices.size, size=max_sample)
            sample = valid_indices[chosen_idx]
        else:
            sample = valid_indices

        best_x = int(sample[0])
        best_val = np.inf
        for xi in sample:
            x = int(xi)
            if k > 0:
                off = x + S_arr
                old_off = c[off]
                max_new_off = int(np.max(old_off)) + 2
                diag_old = int(c[2 * x])
                max_new_diag = diag_old + 1
                max_new = max(max_new_off, max_new_diag)
                einc = 4 * int(np.sum(old_off)) + 4 * k + 2 * diag_old + 1
            else:
                max_new = 1
                einc = 1
            val = max_new * 1000000 + einc
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_x = x

        x = best_x
        if k > 0:
            off = x + S_arr
            changed = np.concatenate((off, np.array([2 * x], dtype=np.int64)), axis=0)
            delta = np.concatenate((np.full(k, 2, dtype=np.int32), np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)), axis=0)
        else:
            changed = np.array([2 * x], dtype=np.int64)
            delta = np.array([1], dtype=np.int32)

        old_counts = c[changed].astype(np.int32).copy()
        c[changed] += delta
        new_counts = c[changed]

        new_bad_list = []
        for pos, oldv, newv in zip(changed, old_counts, new_counts):
            pos = int(pos)
            if (pos % 2) == 0 and int(newv) >= R:
                diag_bad[pos // 2] = True
            if int(oldv) < (R - 1) and int(newv) >= (R - 1):
                new_bad_list.append(pos)

        old_bad_positions = bad_positions.copy() if bad_positions.size else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)
        if old_bad_positions.size:
            xs = old_bad_positions - x
            mask = (xs >= 0) & (xs < n)
            if mask.any():
                np.add.at(forbid, xs[mask], 1)

        S_arr_new = np.concatenate((S_arr, np.array([x], dtype=np.int64)), axis=0)

        if new_bad_list:
            new_bad = np.asarray(new_bad_list, dtype=np.int64)
            bad_off[new_bad] = True
            for tpos in new_bad:
                xs = int(tpos) - S_arr_new
                mask = (xs >= 0) & (xs < n)
                if mask.any():
                    np.add.at(forbid, xs[mask], 1)
            bad_positions = np.concatenate((bad_positions, new_bad), axis=0)

        inS[x] = True
        S_arr = S_arr_new

    return S_arr


def _sparse_obj(z):
    sum_flat = _SPARSE_OPT["sum_flat"]
    n = int(_SPARSE_OPT["n"])
    C = int(_SPARSE_OPT["C"])
    beta = float(_SPARSE_OPT["beta"])
    if sum_flat is None or n <= 1 or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.inf

    m = sum_flat.size ** 0.5
    m = int(round(m))
    w = _softmax(z)
    cw = (w[:, None] * w[None, :]).ravel()
    c = np.bincount(sum_flat, weights=cw, minlength=C).astype(np.float64)
    if c.size < C:
        c = np.pad(c, (0, C - c.size))
    c = c[:C]
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return float(2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * np.log(s)))


def _sparse_grad(z):
    sum_flat = _SPARSE_OPT["sum_flat"]
    n = int(_SPARSE_OPT["n"])
    C = int(_SPARSE_OPT["C"])
    beta = float(_SPARSE_OPT["beta"])
    if sum_flat is None or n <= 1 or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = int(round(sum_flat.size ** 0.5))
    w = _softmax(z)
    cw = (w[:, None] * w[None, :]).ravel()
    c = np.bincount(sum_flat, weights=cw, minlength=C).astype(np.float64)
    if c.size < C:
        c = np.pad(c, (0, C - c.size))
    c = c[:C]

    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        lam = np.zeros(C, dtype=np.float64)
        lam[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        lam = e / s

    A = lam[sum_flat].reshape(m, m)
    h = A.dot(w)
    W = float(np.dot(lam, c))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * w * (h - W)
    return grad


def _sparse_weight_opt(S, n, deadline, min_time_remaining=20.0, label="sparse_opt"):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = S.size
    if m < 2 or n <= 1:
        return None, np.inf
    if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - float(min_time_remaining):
        return None, np.inf

    C = 2 * n - 1
    S2 = S[:, None] + S[None, :]
    sum_flat = S2.ravel().astype(np.int64)

    best_seq = _uniform_sparse_seq(S, n)
    best_sc = _score(best_seq) if best_seq is not None else np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return None, np.inf

    cmax0 = best_sc / max(1.0, 2.0 * n)
    if cmax0 < 1e-300:
        return best_seq, best_sc

    beta_start = max(cmax0 * 0.1, 1e-30)
    beta_end = max(cmax0 * 1e-5, 1e-30)
    if beta_start <= beta_end:
        beta_start = beta_end * 10.0

    log_betas = np.linspace(np.log(beta_start), np.log(beta_end), 14)
    z = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    est = 0.002 + 0.000003 * float(m)

    for beta in log_betas:
        beta = float(beta)
        if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
            continue
        remaining = float(deadline) - time.monotonic() - float(min_time_remaining)
        if remaining < 3.0:
            break

        max_iter = int(min(2500, max(50, remaining / max(1e-9, est * 6.0))))
        _SPARSE_OPT["sum_flat"] = sum_flat
        _SPARSE_OPT["n"] = n
        _SPARSE_OPT["C"] = C
        _SPARSE_OPT["beta"] = beta
        stage_start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            result = minimize(
                _sparse_obj,
                z,
                jac=_sparse_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max_iter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "maxcor": 100,
                    "maxls": 100,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            result = None
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - stage_start
        if max_iter > 0:
            actual = elapsed / max_iter
            if actual > 0.0:
                est = max(est, actual)

        if result is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(result.x)):
            continue
        w = _softmax(result.x)
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[S] = w
        sc = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_seq = a.copy()
            print(f"  [{label}] beta={beta:.3e} score={best_sc:.12f} m={m} n={n}")
        z = np.log(np.maximum(w, 1e-30))
        z = z - float(z.mean())

    return best_seq, best_sc


def _trim_sparse(S):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    if S.size == 0:
        return S, 1
    S = S - int(S.min())
    n = int(S.max()) + 1
    return S, max(1, n)


def _run_sparse_experiment(n_base, deadline, seed):
    n_base = int(n_base)
    if n_base < 64:
        n_base = 64
    n_base = min(n_base, 32768)

    best_seq = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    combos = []
    seen = set()
    for R in (6, 7, 8, 9, 10):
        m_exact = int(np.sqrt(4.0 * n_base * R / 3.0))
        for dm in (2, 0, -2, -4, -8):
            m = m_exact + dm
            if 32 <= m <= 1200:
                key = (R, m)
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    combos.append(key)

    idx = 0
    for R, m_target in combos:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 10.0:
            break
        seed_i = int(seed) + 1000 + 13 * R + 17 * idx
        S = _greedy_sparse_cap(
            n_base,
            m_target,
            R,
            seed=seed_i,
            max_sample=48,
            time_limit=max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, float(deadline) - 5.0),
        )
        S, n2 = _trim_sparse(S)
        idx += 1
        if S.size < 8:
            continue

        seq = _uniform_sparse_seq(S, n2)
        if seq is None:
            continue
        sc = _score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"  [sparse] Rcap={R} m={S.size} n={n2} uniform_score={best_sc:.12f}")

        # Refine one or two promising supports with sparse L-BFGS.
        if sc < 2.0 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 60.0:
            sub_deadline = min(float(deadline) - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            seq2, sc2 = _sparse_weight_opt(S, n2, sub_deadline, min_time_remaining=20.0, label=f"sparse_wR{R}")
            if seq2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                best_seq = seq2.copy()

    return best_seq, best_sc


def _try_update(best_seq, best_score, seq, label=""):
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = _score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        print(f"  UPDATE [{label}] score={float(sc):.12f} n={arr.size}")
        return arr, float(sc)
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = None
    parent_len = 0
    try:
        parent_raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_raw.size > 0:
            parent_raw = np.clip(parent_raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(parent_raw.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = parent_raw
                parent_len = parent_raw.size
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        print(f"Parent loaded: n={parent_len}, score={_score(parent):.12f}")
        best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, parent, "parent")
    else:
        init_seq = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64) / 16.0
        best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, init_seq, "default_init")

    if parent_len >= 16:
        final_n = min(max(parent_len, 16), 32768)
        if parent is not None and parent_len != final_n:
            parent = _interpolate_seq(parent, final_n)
    else:
        final_n = 16384

    # Coarse-to-fine multi-scale construction.
    current = np.ones(4, dtype=np.float64) / 4.0
    best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, current, "n4")

    small_hard = min(hard_deadline - 700.0, t_start + 350.0)
    stage_table = [
        (8, 5.0),
        (16, 10.0),
        (32, 18.0),
        (64, 32.0),
        (128, 58.0),
        (256, 95.0),
        (512, 145.0),
        (1024, 215.0),
        (2048, 300.0),
        (4096, 390.0),
    ]

    for target, offset in stage_table:
        dlocal = min(small_hard, t_start + offset)
        if time.monotonic() >= dlocal:
            break
        if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() < 450.0:
            print("Small stages: reserving time for full-size optimization.")
            break
        a = _interpolate_seq(current, target)
        a, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
            a,
            dlocal,
            min_time_remaining=max(2.0, min(10.0, max(0.0, dlocal - time.monotonic()))),
            max_stages=22,
            label=f"small{target}",
        )
        current = a
        best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, current, f"small{target}")

    # Full-size relaxation from the multi-scale sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 500.0:
        a_full = _interpolate_seq(current, final_n)
        if parent is not None:
            dfull = min(hard_deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 250.0)
        else:
            dfull = min(hard_deadline - 300.0, time.monotonic() + 800.0)
        if dfull - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
            a_full, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                a_full,
                dfull,
                min_time_remaining=max(10.0, min(60.0, max(0.0, dfull - time.monotonic()))),
                max_stages=24,
                label="full_from_mres",
            )
            current = a_full
            best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, current, "full_from_mres")

    # Parent full-size polishing, if available.
    if parent is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 300.0:
        p_full = _interpolate_seq(parent, final_n)
        dparent = min(hard_deadline - 300.0, time.monotonic() + 600.0)
        if dparent - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
            p_full, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                p_full,
                dparent,
                min_time_remaining=max(20.0, min(90.0, max(0.0, dparent - time.monotonic()))),
                max_stages=26,
                label="parent_full",
            )
            best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, p_full, "parent_full")

            # Mixed start: blend parent and multi-scale sequence.
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 200.0:
                cur_full = _interpolate_seq(current, final_n)
                mix = 0.5 * p_full + 0.5 * cur_full
                mix = np.maximum(mix, 0.0)
                ms = float(mix.sum())
                if ms > 0.01:
                    mix = mix / ms
                dmix = min(hard_deadline - 200.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
                if dmix - time.monotonic() > 15.0:
                    mix, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                        mix,
                        dmix,
                        min_time_remaining=max(10.0, min(40.0, max(0.0, dmix - time.monotonic()))),
                        max_stages=20,
                        label="mix",
                    )
                    best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, mix, "mix")

    # Sparse additive-combinatorial starts.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 180.0:
        sparse_end = min(hard_deadline - 150.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
        if sparse_end - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
            s_seq, s_sc = _run_sparse_experiment(final_n, sparse_end, int(seed) + 7919)
            if s_seq is not None and np.isfinite(s_sc):
                best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, s_seq, "sparse")
                # Optional dense relaxation from sparse support.
                if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 120.0 and s_sc < best_score + 0.05:
                    sdense_end = min(hard_deadline - 100.0, time.monotonic() + 50.0)
                    if sdense_end - time.monotonic() > 10.0:
                        sseq_full = _interpolate_seq(s_seq, final_n)
                        sseq_full, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                            sseq_full,
                            sdense_end,
                            min_time_remaining=max(8.0, min(30.0, max(0.0, sdense_end - time.monotonic()))),
                            max_stages=14,
                            label="sparse_dense",
                        )
                        best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, sseq_full, "sparse_dense")

    # Final polishing of best sequence.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10.0:
        dfin = min(hard_deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + 100.0)
        if dfin - time.monotonic() > 8.0:
            fseq, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                best_seq,
                dfin,
                min_time_remaining=max(3.0, min(20.0, max(0.0, dfin - time.monotonic()))),
                max_stages=12,
                label="final_polish",
            )
            best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, fseq, "final_polish")

    # Small perturbations for escape, if time remains.
    if best_seq is not None:
        for sigma in (0.01, 0.04):
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20.0:
                paddr_end = min(hard_deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
                if paddr_end - time.monotonic() > 8.0:
                    pseq = _perturb(best_seq, sigma, rng)
                    pseq, _ = _dense_lbfgs(
                        pseq,
                        paddr_end,
                        min_time_remaining=max(2.0, min(10.0, max(0.0, paddr_end - time.monotonic()))),
                        max_stages=8,
                        label=f"perturb_{sigma}",
                    )
                    best_seq, best_score = _try_update(best_seq, best_score, pseq, f"perturb_{sigma}")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    best_seq = np.clip(np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if best_seq.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        best_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0

    out = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    return out
```
